Local provider-core: reusable provider runtime, with U/A acceptance criteria
Date: 2026-10-18
Status, audited 2026-10-22: local qualification recorded; full goal open

Current completion audit, 2026-10-22
====================================
Original audit runtime source:7c9019eb404a3421471becf22f0b907cbe79e442; image-refresh
record:1fbf06c. This entry updates current status without recertifying earlier
runs. The dated entries below retain their source identities, results and limits.
The full provider-core goal still includes recipient delivery, reusable consumer
contracts and 003's actual API/GUI workflow; package and local-test success alone
cannot close it. One full-dependency distribution remains the installation choice.

Phase requirements and existing evidence
----------------------------------------
P0: concrete owners now exist for the NMRNet session, controller, transport,
    scoped store, exact terminal recovery and host retirement. analysis.py's
    AnalysisSession/PreparedAnalysis contracts and controller.py/execution.py
    preserve preparation, durable execution entry, science and retirement as
    separate operations. Peer-specific policies and recipient witnesses remain.
P1: tools/provider_core_tests.py builds and installs one wheel with the locked
    full dependency closure, runs pip check and executes outside the checkout.
    tests/run_suite.py checks installed origin; contract/test_installation.py
    checks exact wheel contents, forbidden imports and the container marker.
    Python>=3.12 and the full pinned dependency closure are requirements, stated
    directly in packages/provider-core/README.md and requirements/provider-core.md.
    The maintained runtime is Python3.12.12 on Linux x86-64. Other environments
    are unqualified; no runtime migration or wider support is authorized here.
P2: signing, bounded TLS, strict Job input, Upload membership/grant/digest checks
    and socket retirement have installed tests with finite local TLS peers.
    ProviderAPI now implements Hello, configured topology and safe request-ID
    correlation (api.py::publish_hello, _require_api_envelope and ApiFailure).
    This supersedes R2's earlier missing-feature observation. Selected release
    content/vector/route contracts are now qualified at37cf6a1 below. Actual peer
    worker integration and broader consumer/deployment compatibility remain open.
P3: Starter, Executor, Publisher, AttemptStore, AttemptClaims and AttemptRecovery
    own durable effects and exact replay. 003 R36 records successive configured
    Jobs, lost completion acknowledgement, store reopen, explicit byte-identical
    recovery and a third Job with no repeated interpretation or science; its
    scientific ports are recording fixtures. R39 adds installed stopped inspection,
    one exact COMPLETE replay and ordinary idle restart through local TLS. It
    supplied no Job, model request or new science. R40 joins production stopped
    inspection/recovery operations to service-produced outcomes before a third
    Job, still with recording science; its exact evidence is in003. The broader
    connected failure cases in 003's current milestone 3 remain required.
P4: NMRNet interpretation/preparation/inference is connected to the core owners.
    process_reports.py and test_nmrnet_reports.py exercise nested owned causes,
    redaction and failed reporting. Note019 records the current both-stream
    Docker/journald path: bdc2487 removed ordinary stderr/report file dumps.
    Older dated file-sink evidence below describes those historical runs. The
    service tests deliver attributed model-input guidance through the signed
    failure API. 003 R44 now qualifies actual local-GUI rendering/exact download
    of four existing API results (pasted, SMILES N1/N3, XYZ Upload), without new
    Jobs or science. The dev GUI reports no source commit. Commissioner GUI
    submission and remaining connected failure/uncertainty cases stay open.
P5: installed test bindings exercise direct/ZIP source acquisition, immutable
    snapshots, an awaited preparation/prediction split and a binding-only future
    result field. These are separate bounded test seams, not production consumer
    integrations. The real-child retirement fixture proves exact child stop at
    the core seam. These do not qualify another production scientific reader,
    supervisor, process tree, warm reuse or deployment. Required integration
    contracts remain documented; no external repository changes are authorized.

resource observations. Their exact source/result/manifest receipts remain in

and enforced limits remain applicable. No denominator or tolerance is reduced.

54 ordered H/C rows. Its contained receipt review establishes case-level joins
and exact retirement; both original host targets still exited 2. Scripted model
choices do not qualify real-model format interpretation. R38 supplies the stopped
recovery implementation and offline ambiguity-probe mechanics; R39 completes the
bounded installed operator sequence described above. Their capsules and exact
hashes remain in 003, with the original failures and proof limits preserved.

Actual API/live-interpreter evidence also exists: 003 R24 covers pasted input,

predictions match the pinned oracle; generated N1/N3 retain observed provenance
without an independent numerical reference. R28's original overall target failed.
These bounded results do not establish the remaining semantic or GUI coverage.

018 records focused contained results and their receiving-test references for
these changes. The aggregate then passed 375 tests with 11 native-runtime gates
skipped. It did not execute those native witnesses. After the final helper-grant

Do not widen metadata or downgrade dependencies to admit an unqualified runtime.

of the pinned release content, its two signing vectors and selected consumed rules
for all11 provider routes is recorded at37cf6a1 below. This is not complete schema,
recovery-policy or live-deployment compatibility; those require evidence from the
actual intended integration before a broader replacement claim.
R3 remains open: immutable-byte acquisition and verified-file acquisition now
share admission, grants and budgets. The raw transport port is qualified at

see the dated entry below. A production scientific reader/supervisor and its
advertised capacity still require their own integration evidence.
7a98fff joins acquisition and an installed-core snapshot read in an actual
scripted child to core cancellation/retirement and source cleanup, as recorded
below. It does not qualify a production scientific supervisor or reader.
R4: awaited phase-bound conditions, admission/discovery GET recovery, current Hello
recovery and finite-gap active Attempt observation are implemented and locally
qualified in the dated U27 entries below. Production supplies bounded exact START/
terminal recovery and protected phase-delivery continuation. Fixed phase labels
may remain unconfirmed only after original retirement and before a fresh allowed
observation; condition reports still require matching acknowledgement. Bounded
condition recovery is implemented and locally qualified in core and production
wiring, including an actual foreground receiving proof. Owned input-call/selection-time
limits can fail one Job after confirmed retirement and permit later Jobs;
operational/cleanup failures remain service failures. These contracts authorize
no scientific retries or warm reuse. Separately authorized bounded native
prediction recovery is documented in the final 2026-10-23 port entries below;
it does not extend lifecycle replay or introduce warm worker reuse.
R5: scoped stores/shared claims and read-only terminal reconciliation are
implemented; the latter is locally qualified atac70b82/2343e63 below, separately
from TerminalHeld and matching receipts. The service archives exact confirmed bytes
before releasing active slots, only from a clean confirmed inventory; unresolved
obligations block admission. Adopted native channels renew host capacity only
after confirmed retirement/consumption, preserving original request/worker evidence.
History remains retained: physical disk is finite. Attempt-store reserve checks
do not reserve blocks against other writers or cover a separate native filesystem;
native history has no configured reserve/quota or fsynced power-loss guarantee.
Simultaneous independent production lanes and nonempty journal cutover remain
unqualified. Read-only terminal observations stay unresolved without matching receipts.
R6: shared core-owned facts/causes are projected by
provider_core.failure_projection atb8c3cfe below; NMRNet retains its one graph,
redaction, bounds and scientific/recipient policy in process_reports.py.
Protected execution reports now include the acknowledged Job/Attempt references;
pre-start native setup reports include the selected Job only. Exact producer and
receiving evidence is recorded at4d392fa/7c9019e below. Per-run failure.json and
stderr are operator channels. failure.json publication uses a private temporary
file and exclusive link; it does not fsync the file or directory and is not proof
of power-loss durability. AttemptStore has no independent diagnostic-history or
post-replay lookup API. That earlier proposal is not implemented; do not add a
second journal solely to close a checklist row. Actual commissioner/receiver
delivery remains a separate end-to-end requirement.

Production awaited-condition recovery now retains its matching-receipt gate
through temporary API outages. R6's independent scoped diagnostic history remains
unimplemented; current protected
reports and retained lifecycle evidence do not establish that proposed lookup
contract. Neither limit justifies a second journal or general retry framework.
U27/A11 below now identify that concrete need for unattended operation; the
resilience implementation order governs the remaining outage/resource work.

The condition-reporting slice has reached contained qualification: core retains
serialized API effects and phase ordering, and the binding awaits its phase-bound
callable. Repository-check follow-through is qualified at83459e9 below. Unsupported
consumer policies remain explicit limits, not permission to infer readiness or
introduce a new retry/lifetime framework.

003's real-model multi-geometry Upload/format/ambiguity, broader connected faults,
GUI submission/readback and reproducible operator handoff remain open. The selected
ambiguity batch still needs the explicit paid-destination authorization recorded
in R38. Preserve useful contained progress while arranging actual API/model/GUI
prerequisites; no local aggregate or historical status substitutes for those exits.

Packaging priority and installation defaults, 2026-10-20
======================================================
Decision: retain one provider-core distribution with its full runtime dependencies.
The user's final direction, "Full dep, keep it simple", closes the earlier minimal
installation proposal. No minimal group, API-extra split or separate lightweight
distribution is planned. Existing dependency metadata already provides the chosen
behavior and is unchanged. Continue qualifying the full installed package outside
the checkout, including import origins and dependency consistency.

Historical feasibility research for the abandoned option, checked 2026-10-20:
ordinary extras add dependencies; they cannot subtract project.dependencies.
PEP 771 proposes default extras and minimal
installs, but remains Draft. Reference and rationale:
https://peps.python.org/pep-0771/ (Status, Abstract, Specification).
Independent source review confirms the current distinction:
https://packaging.python.org/en/latest/specifications/pyproject-toml/#dependencies
https://packaging.python.org/en/latest/specifications/dependency-groups/#package-building
The first defines runtime/extra requirements; the second excludes dependency
groups from distribution metadata. uv's documented local default-groups setting
(https://docs.astral.sh/uv/concepts/dependencies/#default-groups) controls its
project environment, not the mandatory requirements of an installed wheel.
Rationale for retaining the existing full installation: it already works with
the pinned toolchain and needs no additional distribution or installation mode.
The references above preserve why the minimal option was investigated and dropped;
they do not establish a pending packaging task.

Cross-repository usability workshop, 2026-10-20
=============================================

Decision: keep one local distribution, provider-core, with consumer-owned model
bindings. Reusability means replacing an existing owner without losing its useful
behavior, not importing a fourth copy or making every provider behave like NMRNet.
This section revises the active adoption plan. The 2026-10-18 workshop below is
historical evidence. No sibling migration, runtime upgrade or deployment is
authorized or claimed by this documentation workshop.

Three independent read-only reviewers inspected actual SECS, Magnet and NMRPeak
code, one repository each. The coordinator checked the disputed boundaries against
core and sibling source. All three sibling worktrees were clean. Exact snapshots:
C nmrnet-repro ef2637f4681fbd5267f7544842e5841214826728
M magnet-deploy 600968a5fad8ad0118ea4f42cd7fc1fbfde54aaf
S secs-repro 7356c354368f2355d3ab5cef00cf48c67cea9c2c
P nmrpeak-repro 1697dc84dd1d76ae25b04efc8183fd80614000e5
These aliases are local to this dated section; they do not silently update the
older source aliases below. Source inspection proves design constraints, not
installation, cancellation, native retirement or scientific compatibility.

What belongs where
------------------
Core owns API signing/wire admission, verified Job input, authenticated Upload
acquisition, Attempt records/effects/reconciliation and common chat exchange.
Shared diagnostic rendering and conversation bookkeeping are justified next
candidates: retain safe causes, effect state, budgets and cleanup observations
without importing a provider's scientific vocabulary or publication policy.

Bindings own prompts/tools, interpretation decisions, scientific validation,
prepared worker capabilities, model execution and result meaning. Model-reported
input problems are attributed claims. NMRNet's terminal-report precedence does not
become a universal core policy: NMRPeak deliberately permits another endpoint to
produce an independently admitted candidate. No generic tool registry, universal
worker protocol, model-input schema or exception-to-user-error rule is proposed.

Composition owns endpoint/configuration loading, offering/readiness policy,
scheduling, lane stores, provider-wide claims, native supervision and grants.
Core should issue a Hello operation through the adopted API client; composition
still decides its content, availability transitions, refresh and retry pacing.
Core receives confirmed stop/quiescence observations, never engine authority.

Further extraction candidates need evidence. The verified Job span selection in
C:nmrnet_provider/submission.py:28-41 is generic in shape, but current peer inputs
do not require that operation; keep it local until a real second consumer needs
it. Likewise, NativeDiagnostic's bounded capture can be shared when used by an
installed worker consumer, while RDKit labels/messages remain scientific. Moving
these small helpers alone would not establish cross-repository usability.

Public consumer shape
---------------------
Use documented owner modules, with no startup in provider_core.__init__:
- job_input: FeedInputFacts and VerifiedJobInput; original bytes remain exact.
- signing/transport/api: explicit identity, TLS, bounded I/O and API operations.
- uploads: one Job-scoped acquisition owner and explicitly granted storage.
- analysis: AnalysisSession.prepare/execute/retire and the session-owned
  PreparedAnalysis carrying required opaque prepared identity.
- controller/ownership/attempt_store/publication/recovery: one selected lifecycle and
  one retained obligation, with shared claims across independent execution lanes.
- chat: one bounded Chat Completions exchange; conversation/tool policy remains
  explicit at the consumer boundary.

The async decision stands. Do not add a synchronous lifecycle twin, nested
asyncio.run, blanket to_thread adapters or automatic callable introspection.
Pure verifier/codec leaf adoption may remain synchronous. Real blocking workers
need owned asynchronous channels and confirmed cancellation/retirement paths.
Keep prepared handles local and identity-checked; persisted identity cannot
reconstruct a live handle or authorize scientific replay.

Gaps to close before claiming peer readiness
------------------------------------------
R1. Installed compatibility, not matching package names.
    M/P use Python 3.11 and cryptography 49.0.0; C requires Python >=3.12 and
    cryptography 50.0.0. S advertises >=3.11,<3.13 and locks cryptography 49.0.0.
    These installations cannot be assumed compatible. Qualify the full dependency
    closure even for leaf use such as the Job verifier. The installation decision
    above abandons the earlier stdlib-base/API-extra proposal; compatibility
    qualification remains required.
    Qualify Python 3.11 as well as 3.12 before widening interpreter support.
    Preserve the reason for cryptography 50: requirements/provider-core.md:23-35
    records the 2026-10-18 advisory review, selected Ed25519 scope and rejection
    of an unnecessary affected 49 dependency. Functional compatibility does not
    justify downgrading or widening that security choice. Review current exact-
    version advisories before a later dependency change; full API adoption needs
    an explicitly reconciled peer dependency lock, not two contradictory pins.
    Consumer locks still select exact complete closures. Do not silently upgrade
    sibling runtimes or relax metadata to make pip accept an untested combination.
    Install an exact wheel and record its hash/source revision; 0.1.0 alone does
    not identify bytes. Tests must run outside both checkouts, assert import
    origins and pip check, and require the package's own container marker.

R2. Complete the common API boundary before replacing transport.
    At workshop snapshot C, Hello and configured Nmr-Api-Topology admission were
    missing. The 2026-10-22 audit above records their later implementation;
    peer release/vector qualification remains open. Keep HttpTransport
    protocol-neutral and those operations at ProviderAPI. Preserve
    safe X-Request-ID correlation even if body admission fails. Keep signing,
    endpoint authority, topology and protocol release identity distinct.
    P consumes release sha256:719055db7751605c0e5325ca23bcbe66cde631376d4b2b99523c8e4db75f0d01,
    source 12a53e720fc87e1a4144c76203f8853d5afaf753; C cites API
    6a163b7a9cc1e2c7485b681c0b2b969c7c20e6c8. The coordinator found no diff in
    provider routes, live provider schemas, signing profile, security problem
    definitions or outcome service between those two API revisions. This limited
    comparison is useful evidence, not authentication of the released artifact
    inventory or all consumed rules. Qualify the pinned release/vector boundary.
    Keep core's strict six-field Job envelope/canonical Base64 checks. SECS's
    existing tolerance for a conflicting copied schema field is not a reason
    for a lax compatibility parser; record this deliberate stronger admission.

R3. Share acquisition without forcing large Uploads into memory.
    Core UploadLimits can express S's 128 MiB/source and 256 MiB/Attempt limits;
    the problem is buffering, not a hard-coded small data ceiling. Extend the one
    acquisition owner with an explicitly granted verified-file destination.
    Verify/hash while consuming, account failed bytes, and expose no completed
    file before whole-transfer verification and required I/O settlement. Define
    whether cancellation leaves an incomplete private file and who removes it.
    Bind the worker's later consumption to those same verified bytes. A verified
    pathname alone is insufficient: use an owned immutable/read-only object or
    verify the opened object and consume it without reopening a substitute path.
    Test pathname/symlink substitution and in-place mutation between acquisition
    and consumption; the worker must consume the frozen bytes or refuse them.
    Keep the small immutable-byte result for NMRNet. Do not introduce a second
    downloader/cache/budget. Worker source-file lifetime, ZIP-member traversal,
    decompression limits and scientific parsing remain binding/composition-owned.

R4. Preserve lifecycle policy without weakening effects or stop evidence.
    Core currently stops on any observation failure; M/S tolerate bounded outages
    during already-running work. Specify an explicit observation-gap/deadline
    policy after structured failure classification. Initial admission, mutations,
    malformed replies, rejected authority and actual cancellation are separate;
    an unknown state must never authorize fresh scientific dispatch or replay.
    Add a narrow awaited condition-reporting port for sessions: core still owns
    phase ordering and serialized API effects. A binding must not acquire a raw
    API client, mutate phases independently or bypass durable entry before
    scientific dispatch. This preserves core's existing progress-acknowledgement,
    entry-persistence and fresh-observation order. No unbounded event queue.
    Retry-safe unavailable preparation remains distinct from input failure.

    S currently marks execution before its worker can reject input and return to
    interpretation. Split real worker admission from retrieval/GA first.
    M E01/E02 can report runner_admission_changed after generation dispatch as a
    positive no-computation/refusal claim. The existing Magnet fixture does not
    cover it. Do not reset EXECUTION_ENTERED, infer retry safety from an exception
    or publish bad-input failure. Qualify an exact-input bounded live retry only
    if the runner contract proves no scientific effect and the same frozen input
    remains applicable; if re-interpretation/new prepared identity is needed,
    adoption stays blocked pending an explicit behavioral decision. Restart after
    uncertain dispatch remains observe/reconcile only. G01 is the first M lane.

    Healthy warm reuse needs confirmed quiescence/per-Attempt release; interrupted
    work needs confirmation from its actual supervisor. A closed socket or sent
    RETIRE is not that confirmation. Do not impose NMRNet container shutdown on
    every peer, and do not relabel the existing peer close method as confirmed
    process exit. Their distinct worker protocols remain local unless a separate
    shared component has a real installed consumer and preserves the protocol.

R5. Reconcile existing obligations and independent lanes honestly.
    P HF/CHF cannot share one current core store/controller inventory unchanged:
    a nonterminal record blocks fresh core work and overlapping store calls are
    refused. Start with separate lane-scoped stores and one provider-wide claim
    registry, coordinating the complete startup inventory before lane admission.
    Keep discovery pacing, readiness and shutdown policy in composition.
    S's durable terminal_reconciling/read-only obligation requires the distinct
    TerminalReadRequired/TerminalStateObserved/TerminalReadNotVisible records,
    implemented and locally qualified atac70b82/2343e63 below. Preserve that
    obligation through the actual binding/restart; never restore permission to
    resend, replace terminal bytes or rerun science. Core qualification does not
    establish that the SECS lifecycle has moved to this owner.
    An Attempt-state read does not prove acceptance of the exact retained
    terminal command; keep matching outcome receipts and state observations
    distinct when deciding whether any obligation is discharged.
    Default journal cutover is drain under the old owner, stop/exclude it, retain
    historical evidence separately and admit an empty new scoped store. Refuse
    nonempty cutover. A necessary offline conversion must preserve exact request
    bytes, scope/generation, uncertainty and outstanding obligations, and refuse
    missing facts; P's old records lack some core acknowledgement facts. Do not
    fabricate them. Keep old binding resources, not old competing lifecycle owners.
    Define confirmed-record retention/capacity; do not copy S's journal clearing
    into core implicitly. Qualify complete scientific failure evidence against
    core's 262144-byte limit; never truncate an existing report to fit.

R6. Maximum safe error information through the recipient's real channel.
    Core failures retain .cause/.primary/.cleanup_failures/.api_failure in
    addition to Python chains. P's and S's existing formatters would lose
    that evidence. Provide explicit projections for core-owned failures, keeping
    operation, phase, effect certainty, earlier failures and cleanup observations.
    Share credential redaction before display bounds, bounded traversal, cycle
    handling and explicit omission. Do not enumerate arbitrary object attributes,
    dump raw responses, or claim regex redaction identifies all private science.
    Provider event vocabularies and approved scientific explanations stay local.
    Extend the existing core store's serialized durable I/O with bounded,
    Attempt/generation-bound diagnostic history, independently retained after a
    replay obligation retires. Give this history explicit capacity, retention and
    lookup rules; it must not participate as another replay/claim authority.
    Preserve pre-execution interpreter-unavailable route evidence and useful
    post-retirement lookup. Do not keep the old lifecycle journal merely for its
    diagnostic methods or rely only on evidence written at execution/termination.
    Test developer and operator diagnostics, commissioner Attempt messages and
    receiver result notices separately. A stderr test is not recipient proof.
    Type-only errors are insufficient; raw exception text is not automatically
    public. Persistence/cleanup errors must preserve the primary diagnostic.
    Failure to persist required evidence blocks the dependent scientific or
    publication effect, retaining both the original and storage failure.

Tests first and replacement order
---------------------------------
Use focused contained targets and U26's normative/adversarial split, integration
tests for real connections, contract tests for installation/wire compatibility,
and scenarios for lost replies/restart. No empty Cartesian suite scaffolding.
Each extraction needs a real deletion witness: the replaced analogue disappears
from its selected consumer path; no old/new fallback, second signer, second
downloader or journal authority is left behind. Actual sibling edits remain a
separately authorized cutover; local source-grounded fixtures are not that cutover.

1. Qualify R1's controller installations; reject unsupported runtimes and mixed
   package origins. R2's whole-API replacement separately requires envelope/Hello/
   correlation tests: wrong/missing/duplicate topology, malformed response with
   valid correlation and protocol drift must fail at their owning boundary.
   Prove installation with the full runtime dependency closure under each
   supported, exact locked controller environment.
2. Prove useful leaf adoption: actual SECS Job read -> installed core verifier ->
   unchanged instruction bytes -> existing interpreter boundary. Substitution
   rejects before interpretation. This leaf may retain SECS's existing single
   signed transport and replace only its verifier; unrelated Hello/transport work
   is not a prerequisite. Delete the local verifier rather than keeping fallback.
   For chat, keep one POST after ambiguous
   delivery, explicit retirement, original reasoning/tool IDs, correction context
   and consumer-owned endpoint/report precedence. Do not reproduce unsafe retries
   merely because an older adapter attempted them.
3. Add R6's core failure projections alongside 003's actual input interpreter;
   shared conversation mechanics must support NMRNet's multiple tools and the
   peer's existing report/fallback policies without changing core for chemistry.
   A next tool/result field changes only the binding. New budget/repair/fallback
   choices cannot reset elapsed work or replay a completed tool effect.
   Persist pre-execution unavailability, then retire the replay obligation and
   prove scoped diagnostic lookup still works. Exercise nested primary/cleanup
   causes, credential-prefix canaries, multibyte bounds and evidence-write failure
   through the actual consumer formatter and public result/Attempt projections.
4. Complete R2 before replacing a whole API client. Prove G01's real prepare/
   predict split with its actual contracts and a controlled
   backend. Prove P with both installed distributions and simultaneous HF/CHF;
   no nested event loop or blocking runner call on the control loop. Prove S with
   streamed verified files and worker-side preparation before one scientific run.
   Include outage/recovery policy, condition messages, rejected preparation,
   cancellation with real stop confirmation and a lost terminal acknowledgement
   followed by processor-free exact replay. E01/E02 follow their R4 decision.
5. Only then cut over a peer's selected owners and remove their analogues, with
   explicit old-journal disposition and source/wheel/lock/runtime receipts.
   Numerical model qualification, workload capacity and deployed API/GUI proof
   remain distinct exits. No broad/costly suite is part of routine plan review.

Precise sources and rationale
----------------------------
Primary peer source entrypoints:
https://github.com/numpde/magnet-deploy/blob/600968a5fad8ad0118ea4f42cd7fc1fbfde54aaf/models/g_01_nmr_forward/provider/magnet_provider_bindings/g_01_nmr_forward/processor.py
https://github.com/numpde/secs-repro/blob/7356c354368f2355d3ab5cef00cf48c67cea9c2c/src/secs_inference/provider/analysis_run.py
https://github.com/numpde/nmrpeak-repro/blob/1697dc84dd1d76ae25b04efc8183fd80614000e5/nmrpeak_provider/attempt_lifecycle.py
For each repository alias above use https://github.com/numpde/REPO/blob/REV/PATH;
the following exact revision/path/line anchors explain each decision:
- C:packages/provider-core/{pyproject.toml,src/provider_core/analysis.py:52-84,
  src/provider_core/controller.py:102-116,src/provider_core/execution.py:237-254,
  src/provider_core/attempt_store.py:127-143}: actual installation/session/poll/
  store constraints, rather than an assumed generic provider interface.
- C:src paths below are relative to packages/provider-core/src/provider_core/:
  api.py:170-223; uploads.py:39-67,186-247; transport.py:109-119;
  attempt_record.py:162-177,247-273; recovery.py:72-104.
  Rationale: envelope/correlation gaps, memory handoff, evidence capacity and
  durable reconciliation obligations are current implementation facts.
