Workshop acceptance: no module is moved merely because it looks generic. Close
the real peer contract gaps without importing their scientific semantics or
lowering their meaningful safeguards. Current installed/local NMRNet proof and
the scripted Magnet adaptation remain useful, explicitly limited evidence.

Review closure: all three peer reviewers challenged the synthesis against actual
source and U/A intent. Revisions added consumed-file byte binding, independent
leaf adoption, accurate progress ordering, the dependency security rationale,
explicit scoped diagnostic lifetime and exact-receipt/state distinctions. Two
subsequent unchanged-design rounds from all three returned no actionable signal.
The final module-list spelling is attempt_store, matching the installed owner.
No tests were added or run for this documentation workshop; runtime assertions
above are source observations or explicitly future qualification obligations.

Prepared-input identity follow-up, 2026-10-19
===========================================

003's interpreted-source route needs the selected candidate identity before model
dispatch, in addition to original Job identity. Core analysis.PreparedAnalysis now
provides that session-owned handle with required ProtectedEvidence. The executor
writes the identity through the existing execution-entry CAS and waits for settled
durability and active-state observation before executing. Live authority remains
in the session; restoring bytes grants no replay. A fresh store transition cannot
omit identity. Historical acknowledged.v1 records remain readable and encode v1;
new identity-bearing entries use strict v2, also within existing terminal envelopes.
NMRNet is the actual consumer; sibling adoption fixtures were migrated without
editing siblings. Required evidence bounds and future-store reservations cover
both input identity and terminal evidence. See 003's dated follow-up and focused
prepared-identity lanes for proof; Upload/interpreter/service composition remains
open and this prerequisite does not close P4/P5 or 003.

Decision and scope
==================

The maintainer requests a local package named provider-core, intended eventually
to replace the corresponding provider infrastructure in sibling repositories.
The import namespace is provider_core. NMRNet is its first real consumer.
There is no dependency on magnet-provider-core and no request to modify Magnet.
The maintainer then explicitly required planning and U/A audit before building.
That planning step was documentation/read-only investigation. The maintainer has
subsequently authorized implementing the package tests-first; progress is below.

This decision supersedes 003's proposed shared-Magnet extension. It does not
cancel 003's three input routes, interpretation loop, scientific constraints,
container requirements or API/GUI end-to-end completion criteria. Sibling
migrations, external publication and deployment remain separate authorization.

Success is not a renamed fourth copy of a provider implementation. The installed
package must own the common behavior that the NMRNet workflow actually consumes,
with independently checked adoption contracts for Magnet, SECS and NMRPeak.
Their present implementations are evidence, not a universal behavioral template.

Evidence and rationale
======================

013 owns U01-U26, A01-A10 and the historical X01-X07 scope dispositions. Read their
full intent, especially U23: useful errors must reach each authorized end-user,
not merely stderr, while secrets and unauthorized scientific information do not.
014 pins the following source aliases; references here use those revisions:
M magnet-deploy c5813ee35cfbb1c98a5cc5d20a94787271f26486
S secs-repro 34f5915c1534278bbf61b4c6592afa44cf94ab08
P nmrpeak-repro 1697dc84dd1d76ae25b04efc8183fd80614000e5
I nmr-api-v1 6a163b7a9cc1e2c7485b681c0b2b969c7c20e6c8
G nmr-gui-v1 d432668a137e860e1bf37bf242f792ec07f01533
Resolve paths via the exact GitHub tree URLs recorded in 014, not moving HEAD.

- M:magnet_provider/{analysis_contract,analysis_execution,provider_protocol}.py:
  analysis outcomes and transport/commit certainty are separate responsibilities.
  Preserve that distinction rather than importing Magnet's scientific policies.
- M:magnet_provider/job_input.py: authenticate exact Job text against feed facts
  before interpretation. Original instructions differ from prepared model input.
- M:magnet_provider/provider_http.py and P:nmrpeak_provider/{provider_signing,
  provider_https,http_response}.py: signing and response ownership precedents.
  S:src/secs_inference/provider/socket_deadline.py: whole-exchange deadlines and
  retirement of deadline helpers; a library read timeout is insufficient evidence.
- S:src/secs_inference/provider/{job_api,analysis_run,upload_download}.py:
  Job membership -> temporary read grant -> verified bytes, with no model-granted
  access. Keep generic source authority in core; format selection stays in binding.
- S:src/secs_inference/provider/{execution,diagnostics}.py and
  P:nmrpeak_provider/{attempt_lifecycle,diagnostic_text,provider_events}.py:
  durable effects and recipient-specific diagnostics are related but distinct.
  Do not adopt one sibling's retries, scientific correction or journal schema
  merely because it contains more machinery.
- I:nmr_api/provider/routes.py, provider/job_upload_set_read_response.py,
  operations/provider_job_upload_set_read_service.py,
  operations/provider_job_upload_read_capability.py and
  wire/upload_read_capability_response.py: existing API authority and wire shapes.
  G:app/src/features/workspace/ColumnExecutionAttempt.tsx and result-presentation/
  ResultPresentation.tsx: actual commissioner/receiver display boundaries.

M:pyproject.toml demonstrates a standalone distribution. Its dependency pins are
candidates for comparison, not automatically the new package's dependency policy.
S's AGPL notice must be retained if its code is actually reused; do not treat
NMRNetplusplus's MIT license as licensing this new package. Record actual source
adaptation and applicable notices when implementing; publication licensing is a
separate explicit decision, not a reason to invent a substitute framework.

Finished ownership and enforceable invariants
============================================

1. API server owns authorization, eligibility, membership, expiry and committed
   Attempt state. Core authenticates exchanges and binds returned facts; it cannot
   create authority from text, a local flag, an interpreter decision or a receipt.
2. Core is the sole NMRNet owner of signing, bounded authenticated API transport,
   verified Job input, Job-bound Upload access, generic Attempt effects and exact
   terminal reconciliation. No second signer, downloader or replay owner in binding.
3. Binding owns scientific intent, typed interpretation tools, supported formats,
   geometry generation/conversion, scientific admission, model execution and result
   meaning. Core does not import NMRNet, RDKit, Torch, sibling packages or repo tools.
   XYZ and other chemistry readers do not move into provider-core.
4. Host/container composition owns actual network, filesystem, credential, resource
   and worker-retirement grants. Core consumes narrow execution/control ports and
   blocks new work when retirement is unconfirmed; it never receives an engine
   socket or implements a competing deployment manager.
5. Cross-module records distinguish original Job bytes, selected Upload bytes,
   prepared executable bytes and exact terminal bytes. Verified immutable bytes
   are the bytes consumed; paths do not become later unchecked authority.
6. One active Attempt per execution owner; NMRNet initially has one owner. Sibling
   composition may run multiple independent lanes with one shared claim authority
   excluding duplicate ownership and recovery races. Core adds no scheduler or
   process-global singleton. Determinate rejection, unavailable operation, potentially
   committed effect, observed committed effect, and failed cleanup are different
   states. No implicit queue, blind retry, science replay or generic catch-to-user-
   error conversion. Retry is an explicit bounded policy, not a diagnosis.
7. Errors carry the maximum safe useful cause, operation/phase, effect state and
   next action through the intended recipient's actual channel. Diagnostic-storage
   or cleanup failure cannot erase the primary cause or misreport commitment.
8. The package installs and executes independently of this checkout. Each adopted
   behavior has one owner and a real consumer. A sibling migration later replaces
   its analogue rather than wrapping old and new owners with silent fallback.

Package and composition
=======================

Proposed source layout: packages/provider-core/pyproject.toml and
packages/provider-core/src/provider_core/. This is a separately installable local
distribution, not a provider-named directory exported through root PYTHONPATH.
Keep public contracts small: verified source values, Job-scoped source access,
analysis outcome/effect values and an analysis processor boundary. Introduce a
port only where an actual integration or alternate implementation needs one.
No plugin registry, generic DAG, universal configuration language or event bus.

Layout workshop, 2026-10-18
--------------------------
Extraction follow-up, 2026-10-20: provider_core.chat now owns the shared Chat
Completions carrier (003 records the reviewed move and local qualification).
Shared conversation mechanics belong in core as the NMRNet tool consumer is
connected; prompts, tool implementations and scientific admission remain local.
Worker framing/channel ownership and host receipt observation are further shared
candidates, but their native installation is an unresolved part of extraction.
The full-dependency decision above supersedes the earlier stdlib-only worker
installation proposal. A complete worker move must install the built wheel and
its full locked runtime dependencies in every consuming native runtime, update
source closures/guards and qualify the changed image. Current worker images and
locks are unchanged. Neither installing an incomplete distribution with --no-deps
nor mounting core sources is an acceptable shortcut. Controller-only retirement
can be extracted independently. Keep host engine control in tooling;
do not call a relocated controller test proof of the native installed boundary.
Current evidence: pyproject.toml dependency metadata; tools/provider_core_tests.py
build/install lifetime; containers/{geometry,inference}/Dockerfile runtime locks;
tools/worker_qualification.sh direct retirement/protocol grants; tools/baseline.sh
worker-parity entrypoint. These are local repository sources, inspected 2026-10-20.

The following is the intended layout as slices acquire real behavior, not an
instruction to generate empty files. The earlier conversational contracts.py
proposal is replaced by types beside their owning behavior. Public consumers
import documented owner modules; __init__.py stays small and has no startup effects.

packages/provider-core/
  pyproject.toml                 Distribution, build inputs and runtime dependencies.
  README.md                      Public API, ownership, containment and test guidance.
  src/provider_core/
    __init__.py
    job_input.py                 Verified Job instructions and their source identity.
    signing.py                   Signing inputs and authenticated request construction.
    transport.py                 Bounded exchanges, response consumption and retirement.
    chat.py                      Chat Completions carrier; consumer owns tools and prompts.
    api.py                       API wire operations and their explicit effect outcomes.
    uploads.py                   Job membership refresh/grants and verified consumption.
    analysis.py                  Preparation/execution boundary and outcome handoff.
    attempts.py                  Per-owner serial decisions and effect ordering.
    recovery.py                  Exact retained-terminal reconciliation policy.
    attempt_store.py             Durable records, atomic writes and retention capacity.
    diagnostics.py               Safe diagnostic context and recipient projections.
    container_guard.py           Shared execution-policy guard.
  tests/
    README.md                    Suite purposes, contained invocation and evidence limits.
    unit/normative/
    unit/adversarial/
    contract/
    integration/
    scenarios/
    fakes/

Types stay local: job_input owns verified instructions; analysis owns processor
outcomes; api owns wire/effect results; attempt_store owns persisted records.
Do not create a central types/contracts/utils bucket or mirror those definitions
in the NMRNet adapter. Extract a shared type only when it has one clear owner and
actual cross-module consumers. Errors likewise start beside their operation;
diagnostics preserves and projects their context, not a universal exception registry.

Dependencies point from orchestration to effects: attempts uses analysis, source
access, API operations and recovery; recovery uses the store and API reconciliation.
API operations use signing/transport. Upload access uses API membership/grants and
bounded store transport without forwarding provider credentials. The store does
not call the API or decide retry policy. Signing/transport do not import lifecycle
or science. Dependency cycles require correcting ownership, not deferred imports.

The consumer composition supplies the processor, policy, credentials and narrow
worker-control boundary. Its installed NMRNet binding imports provider_core; core
never imports nmrnet_provider. No generic executable/CLI, deployment manager or
plugin discovery is needed to make this library reusable. Host engine control and
actual filesystem/network/resource grants stay in repository composition.

The analysis boundary separates preparation from execution: binding returns an
admitted execution handle; core confirms required progress and durably records
execution entry before invoking that handle. Core cannot forge or reconstruct
session-owned handles. A monolithic callback that marks science entered before
interpretation/validation is insufficient. Binding owns generation compatibility
for pre-execution recovery. Terminal replay needs neither processor nor runner.
Execution-model decision, accepted by the maintainer on 2026-10-18: one async
provider orchestration implementation. The application composition owns the event
loop and task lifetimes. Core awaits API/store transport, interpreter/worker
protocols, readiness, progress, execution and recovery. Pure parsing, validation
and state decisions remain ordinary synchronous functions. RDKit and inference
remain synchronous inside isolated scientific workers, never on the control loop.

Rationale: cancellation, expiry and shutdown must remain observable while external
operations wait. Magnet already exposes awaited ports; SECS and NMRPeak need
explicit controller integration changes. Their synchronous owners may already use
threads; that does not establish compatibility with async cancellation or ownership.
P5 must qualify their async integration rather than merely matching data types.
No sync lifecycle twin, nested event loops, callable-result introspection or
automatic wrapping of arbitrary synchronous processors in threads.

P0 names the task owner, stop path and confirmation evidence for every blocking
effect. Inherently blocking durable storage may use a narrowly owned, serialized
I/O executor if needed; this is an explicit store implementation, not a general
sync-consumer adapter. Bound outstanding writes, preserve write order and await
their actual completion before dependent effects or store reuse. Cancelling an
await cannot cancel a filesystem write or prove that it failed. Storage failure
and uncertain persistence block unsafe advancement. No untracked fire-and-forget
task or thread may outlive its owning operation without retained recovery ownership.

Cancellation is a request to stop, not evidence that an API mutation, worker or
store operation stopped. Preserve each operation's effect certainty, execute bounded
cleanup under its named owner, and await required retirement confirmation before
reuse. Cleanup failure cannot replace the primary cause. Expiry/shutdown must not
be mapped to bad scientific input. Independent lanes may proceed only where their
claims/resources are independent; NMRNet initially admits one execution owner.
The outcome handoff also distinguishes successful result bytes from a binding-
classified failure projection and required protected evidence. Persist required
failure evidence before publishing its terminal report; storage failure blocks
that effect without hiding the primary cause. Product outcome vocabulary stays
in binding, including SECS cannot_analyse and no_starting_candidates.

One Job-scoped source owner refreshes authenticated membership observations,
invalidates stale acquired selections and acquires bytes through fresh grants.
Binding decides reinspection/reselection after an observed change; it does not
own a second downloader/cache. Freeze the identity of consumed bytes. The API
exposes no membership generation or atomic execution freeze: do not claim that
changes after the last membership observation are atomically excluded. P2 tests
removal after inspection, changed/unchanged membership after grant failure and
pending-hash finalization. Defensive same-reference metadata-change fixtures do
not establish that the server permits replacing finalized content.

Package metadata declares its actual runtime dependency requirements; the existing
repository acquisition owners maintain the separately reviewed exact core lock and
advisory evidence. Do not add a package-local build/download/test shell framework.
Installed tests must import the built distribution outside the checkout, with no
source-path injection. Tests, fakes, credentials, retained Attempts and scientific
artifacts are excluded from the runtime wheel. Required runtime resources are
explicit package data and checked in installed-closure tests; no resource directory
or duplicated schema/prompt is added without a real runtime consumer. In particular,
NMRNet interpretation prompts and geometry fixtures remain with the binding.

Evolution checks for this layout: replacing HTTP transport must not change Attempt
policy; replacing durable storage must preserve exact replay semantics; adding a
NMRNet format must not change core; changing an API field belongs at the wire
boundary and its consumers. These are ownership review questions, not an excuse
to implement multiple backends or a public interface for every internal function.

Concrete modules follow behavioral owners during implementation, not an up-front
empty module inventory. First useful behavior: verified Job text from exact
authenticated feed/response facts, consumed by the NMRNet submission adapter.
Then signed transport and complete Job-scoped Upload acquisition; then Attempt
lifecycle/publication and its required durable reconciliation. Packaging alone is
not completion. Do not ship an unused verifier as the finished shared package.

Configuration is admitted once per run: provider/API identity, destination trust,
Upload-store origin, deadlines/work limits and immutable implementation identity.
The caller supplies product policy explicitly. No NMRNet limits, absent-nucleus
semantics, solvent vocabulary or retry assumptions become core defaults.
Credentials never cross into the analysis/preparation worker. The scoped Upload
port takes an Upload reference; the interpreter cannot supply a different Job.

All development, building, installing and running remains contained. Core owns a
portable runtime guard rather than importing tools.container_guard; guarded entry
points reject unsupported host execution. The marker checks shared containment
policy; it is not proof of isolation or NMRNet-specific scientific policy.
Test real container grants and denial separately. Repository
tools/checks retain their current guards; new package sources enter guard, lint,
cache, source and build-context inventories explicitly, without blanket exemptions.
Use existing acquisition/build/test owners where applicable. Dependency acquisition
requires explicit interface selection; no ambient interface fallback. Independently
review current advisories for exact versions in the complete core dependency
closure before installing it. Keep it separate
from the qualified inference and preparation dependency locks.

Test-first implementation sequence (requires review first)
==========================================================

Test layout implements 013 U26, not a flat mirror of source modules.
Sources: M:tests/{unit,contract,integration}/ and Makefile targets test/unit,
test/contract, test/integration; P:tests/{unit,contract,integration,repository,
fakes,live,model_behavior}/ and Makefile. Revisions are pinned above. The distinction
keeps local behavior, external compatibility and composed lifecycle proof visible.

packages/provider-core/tests/
  unit/normative/   Required behavior, including specified rejection and ordering.
  unit/adversarial/ Malformed, hostile or fault-injected boundary challenges.
  contract/      API wire/signature compatibility and consumer boundary contracts.
  integration/  Composed source/Attempt/recovery flows, finite peers, installed use.
  scenarios/     Recognizable multi-step consumer workflows and recovery stories.
  fakes/         Shared controlled peers/stores only where tests need them.

The normative/adversarial split follows I:tests/unit/{normative,adversarial}/ and
G:tests/unit/{normative,adversarial}/. I also uses integration_tests/{normative,
adversarial}/: these categories express test intent, not merely test granularity.
S:tests/input/README.md and tests/input/{normative,adversarial,scenarios,adapters}/
provide the domain-suite precedent. In particular, adversarial/test_source_boundaries.py
checks foreign references, duplicate archive members and storage faults with no
downstream inference; scenarios/test_scientific_jobs.py exercises interacting inputs
and corrections at a worker boundary, explicitly not an API/GUI end-to-end claim.
Use these precise sources at the S/I/G revisions above.

Normative is not synonymous with happy path: required refusal, precedence and
atomicity are normative contracts too. Adversarial tests challenge those contracts
with tampering, substitutions, exhaustion and interrupted effects. Scenario tests
exercise complete meaningful stories (e.g. lost publication reply, restart, exact
reconciliation without another analysis). Integration tests focus on the actual
connections between owners, such as signed transport and store consumption.
Place each test under its primary purpose; do not duplicate it across categories.
Use normative/adversarial subgroups within other suites only where actual coverage
benefits. Adapter-specific tests belong with the boundary they qualify; S's adapters
directory does not require a new core adapter layer. Repo-checks, system/e2e and
model-behavior lanes stay separate from these package behavior categories.
Fixture data belongs beside its owning suite, with precise provenance for derived
examples. Within each layer, group by behavior; do not create empty directories.
Repository conventions and actual container/launcher grants remain repository-level
checks. NMRNet interpretation/model behavior and live API/GUI end-to-end tests stay
outside the shared package. Do not add model_behavior/live directories to core
merely to copy the sibling tree. Focused contained Make targets must preserve the
unit/contract/integration/scenarios distinction, allow focused normative/adversarial
selection, and select provider-core explicitly.

This layout clarification follows the maintainer's correction after the design
audit; it changes test organization, not the behavioral acceptance obligations.

P0. Freeze owner/consumer contracts from the pinned API and sibling code.
    Specify exact API types, bounds, state transitions and recipient projections.
    Specify async operation/task ownership, cancellation and shutdown ordering,
    aggregate deadlines and explicit treatment of blocking persistence. Choose
    concrete existing async-capable I/O dependencies during closure review, not
    an additional scheduling framework. Scientific workers remain isolated.
    Freeze expected populations before iteration: the retained 002 artifact's four
    fixture identities, 137 outputs and 64 historical positions; separately the
    three 003 routes (SMILES+N, one pasted geometry, instructed Upload). Missing
    cases fail qualification rather than shrinking its denominator.
    Compare differences explicitly; do not erase them into a common denominator.
    Exit: the end-to-end NMRNet composition names every effect owner, including
    host retirement and exact terminal retention, with no unowned integration seam.
P1. Installable package plus exact verified Job-input behavior and NMRNet adapter.
    Establish U26 discovery and contained selection with the first actual tests:
    normative source/identity contracts, adversarial substitutions, and installed
    package integration. Missing selected suites/tests must fail, not pass empty.
    Write boundary failures first: wrong Job/schema/digest/length, duplicate JSON,
    invalid UTF-8/base64 and over-limit replies. Failed verification must cause no
    interpretation/preparation call. Preserve exact accepted instructions.
    Build/install offline in a clean contained prefix; run outside the checkout
    with isolated imports. Assert distribution metadata, complete package resources
    and imported origins. A source-tree import is not installed-package proof.
P2. Signed exchanges and Job-bound Upload acquisition, end to end with finite peers.
    Exact method/authority/path/query/body signing; list membership, fresh grant,
    allowed store origin, no redirects or provider credentials at store, length/
    digest checks, private staging and no exposure of partial bytes. Include total
    and per-source budgets while failed/trickling responses are consumed.
    Test the real transport and socket retirement, not only mocked HTTP functions.
    Cancel during connect/headers/body and Upload transfer; verify deadline/stop
    observation while a peer stalls, socket retirement and no partial-byte handoff.
P3. Attempt lifecycle and terminal-effect recovery with one active owner.
    Start/progress/terminal/readback paths share one protocol owner. Specify durable
    state from exact replay obligations; reserve bounded retention capacity before
    accepting obligations that need it. Inject response loss before/after each
    commit, restart, expiry, disk failure, conflicting owner and failed retirement.
    Reconcile exact terminal bytes; never rerun science to resolve uncertain delivery.
    Include pending-start uncertainty and prepared-versus-execution-entered states.
    Qualify composition-supplied claim exclusion across independent lanes/recovery;
    NMRNet still deploys one lane. Do not impose NMRNet's lane count on core users.
    Inject cancellation before/after start, progress, durable execution entry and
    terminal publication. An unfinished write/mutation stays owned and unresolved
    until observed/reconciled; no duplicate write, science replay or early reuse.
    Check stop/expiry observation while worker I/O or persistence is delayed and
    verify bounded shutdown preserves retained obligations and primary diagnostics.
P4. NMRNet composition and recipient-visible diagnostics.
    Core handles orchestration; NMRNet supplies interpretation/preparation/inference.
    Exercise success, user correction, unavailable service, uncertain effect,
    oversized reason, nested secret-bearing cause and diagnostic persistence failure.
    Inspect developer evidence, operator recovery output, commissioner Attempt view
    and receiver result/download. Rendering tests must retain safe causes and limits.
    Apply the interpreter and recipient contracts below, through actual bindings.
P5. Reuse qualification and full 003 workflow.
    Build three source-grounded consumer contract fixtures: Magnet implementation
    binding, SECS source-driven analysis, NMRPeak direct/interpreted request outcomes.
    Show each can express its own product policies without importing sibling code
    or changing core for chemistry. Include a next-change test (new product result
    field or tool) that changes only the binding, and an API protocol change that
    fails explicit compatibility admission rather than silently dropping fields.
    Magnet's fixture uses awaited ports. SECS/NMRPeak fixtures must demonstrate
    explicit async controller/worker integration, including cancellation and
    retirement; synchronous source compatibility alone cannot pass this witness.
    These are adoption evidence, not claims that siblings have migrated. Run real
    NMRNet/API/GUI transactions for all 003 routes and failure/restart cases before
    claiming 003 closed. Later sibling migration requires an explicit consumer
    revision, qualification, cutover and deletion of displaced authority.
    Before any future cutover, explicitly drain/refuse nonempty old journals or
    qualify an offline conversion preserving owner, exact command, uncertainty and
    recovery disposition. Never start an empty new store over retained obligations
    or allow old and new recovery owners to run together. Reader/archive tools
    must change with their store. Migration is still separately authorized.

Concrete integration acceptance contracts
========================================

Retirement: composition confirms that the exact owned execution has stopped after
interruption, with the required grant cleanup. Successful quiescence and permitted
warm reuse are distinct from stopping a worker. SECS/Magnet retain successful warm
workers; a ready response is not evidence of stopping an interrupted execution.
NMRNet's initial container-retirement implementation confirms its allocated exact
container stopped and its grants were released. A shared name, timed-out wait or
stop request is insufficient. Core receives the bounded result, never an engine
handle. P0 names the controller, execution identity and actual confirmation scope;
P3 injects wrong/stale identity, supervisor loss and failed confirmation. Unsafe
work blocks reuse until confirmed stopped; successful warm reuse also requires
observed quiescence and completed per-Attempt cleanup under explicit consumer
policy. No general deployment interface is introduced.

Interpreter (NMRNet binding, not core policy): preserve original Job instructions
separately from admitted executable bytes. Test direct and interpreted routes,
bounded endpoint/repair calls and an overall deadline; malformed direct JSON must
not silently become prose. Distinguish model-reported input problems, deterministic
admission rejection, endpoint failure and indeterminate execution. Interpreter
egress uses an explicitly trusted destination and its own credential grant; source
or model output cannot widen Upload authority. Include credential/private-data
canaries and ensure rejection cannot start preparation. 003/016 own format,
conversion, native-work and result-size limits; these witnesses exercise them
through the real binding rather than duplicating scientific policy in core.
Following 014 W03/W04, test configured endpoint order and bounded fallback; explain
to operators and commissioners what request material goes to external interpreters.
Fallback must not override a terminal source-problem report. Test that such a
report ends interpretation without calling another endpoint or starting preparation;
operational fallback must preserve the stated deadline and effect-certainty rules.
Include wrong/ambiguous-unit fixtures through binding conversion, proving core
neither infers nor repairs units. Account failed and partial preparation work
against the same admitted work/time budget as successful preparation.

Recipient projections must use actual supported channels, grounded in I and G:
- Commissioner: terminal providerFailure.code/message, rendered by
  G:app/src/features/workspace/ColumnExecutionAttempt.tsx, carry safe useful cause
  and next action. In-progress providerCondition.code selects the GUI catalog's
  message/next_action, with observedAt; it cannot carry arbitrary per-Job detail.
  Do not turn uncertain delivery into a false terminal failure to get richer text.
- Receiver: the authorized result/download carries scientific limitations and
  provenance, including the existing scaler warning. Never invent a successful
  result for a failed or unresolved Attempt.
- Operator: protected recovery output preserves operation, causal chain, exact
  effect certainty and permissible retry/reconciliation action.
- Developer: protected diagnostic evidence retains safe causal detail and source
  identity needed to reproduce the failure, without raw credentials or input dumps.
P0 names concrete API wire fields, result renderer and protected diagnostic/recovery
store or sink for every supported path. P4 covers normal rejection, unavailable
service, uncertain delivery, cleanup failure and diagnostic-store failure on their
meaningful recipient paths. Assert safe cause, effect truth, retry disposition and
next action where applicable; secret canaries must stay absent. Storage/cleanup
failure remains secondary evidence and cannot erase the primary cause. Do not
invent a generic projection framework or a meaningless Cartesian test matrix.

Reuse evidence qualifies a design for adoption. Only an actual sibling cutover,
with qualification and removal of its old owner, proves shared production use.

U/A conformance ledger: owner and required witness
=================================================

Every row is a planned acceptance obligation, not a passing result. Core, binding
and composition obligations all matter; moving a rule outside core is not exemption.

U01 binding: one existing scientific callable; adapter spy plus real 003 path prove
    no parallel predictor or checkpoint-specific scientific implementation.
U02 binding: ordered interpretation/preparation/admission/inference; a failure at
    each boundary proves later scientific effects do not occur.
U03 core+binding: Job/source/prepared/result identities travel with their selected
    bytes; swap, reorder and stale-selection fixtures reject or preserve identity.
U04 binding+core compatibility: scientific semantics stay in binding, wire/package
    compatibility in core admission; shape-compatible but wrong-schema cases fail.
U05 core source owner: hash and consume identical immutable bytes; mutate/reopen
    fixtures prove no checked-path/unchecked-read substitution.
U06 qualification: exact producer revisions, populations and installed closure;
    assert P0's expected fixture and route sets before iteration; portable evidence
    names missing cases rather than reducing a denominator.
U07 composition: real signed exchange, actual worker and API/GUI route tests;
    component mocks cannot substitute for those completion witnesses.
U08 core lifecycle: explicit uncertain/committed/rejected outcomes; response-loss,
    cleanup and restart fault injection preserves effect truth and safe next action.
U09 core+binding: declaration/admission/acquisition/execution are separate effects;
    malformed input and foreign Upload cause no download or model work.
U10 core contracts: named immutable cross-module values; mutation and repr canaries
    distinguish empty, unavailable and unresolved without exposing protected data.
U11 package: installed-origin and exact-resource inventory checks, forbidden imports
    and blocked effect tests; passive source inspection alone does not qualify it.
U12 launcher: literal hierarchical Make inputs and protected aliases; real Make
    hostile/ambient-value tests show no expansion or unauthorized engine call.
U13 transports/preparation: consumed byte/time/work budgets include failures and
    retries; trickle, partial read, aggregate exhaustion and oversized body tests.
U14 composition+core admission: retire exact owned worker/transport before reuse;
    interrupted work requires confirmed stop; successful warm reuse follows the
    explicit quiescence contract. Replaced identity and failed-retirement tests
    leave admission blocked.
U15 review/checks: judge requirements by intent; focused properties cover actual
    effects, with no proof-token registry or implementation-shaped test substitute.
U16 owner docs/code: precise revision/path/reference and adaptation rationale;
    reviewer traces each borrowed behavior to source and distinguishes runtime proof.
U17 recipient text: plain blocked operation, safe cause and next action; independent
    reader explains the actual user-facing failure without internal shorthand.
U18 delivery: coherent audited slices and explicit-path commits; status distinguishes
    implemented, installed, locally qualified and deployed; no unrequested push.
U19 artifacts: keep exact reproducibility evidence and enduring rationale; retention
    disposition identifies why each bundle remains and when scratch can be removed.
U20 package acquisition: review full dependency lock/advisories; no automatic lock
    refresh treated as deterministic regeneration or unreviewed producer update.
U21 qualification: version-matched independent observations; changing dependency,
    implementation or protocol version blocks stale-reference qualification.
U22 notes/guide: supersede active Magnet-reuse instructions and mark historical
    observations; review finds no mixed current architectures in 003/016/README.
U23 diagnostics: safe causes reach actual developer/operator/commissioner/receiver
    channels, including nested causes and delivery failure; secret canaries never
    leak, safe useful context remains, uncertainty and retry disposition stay distinct.
U24 accepted limitations: maintain explicit executable cases for known limitations;
    NMRNet's scaler warning reaches its result/diagnostics, not just a local log.
U25 test lanes: focused cheap checks routinely; expensive real-capacity and full
    workflow witnesses in explicit contained lanes and run before their claims.
U26 package test organization: normative/adversarial unit tests, contract,
    integration and scenario suites preserve their distinct purposes; repository
    checks and live/scientific proof stay in their owning lanes. Verify focused
    contained discovery includes the intended tests and rejects empty selections.
    Shared fakes and provenance-bearing fixtures serve tests, never runtime imports.

U27 service+operation+resource owners: transient outages recover automatically with
    configurable capped backoff, bounded exchanges and responsive shutdown; exact
    replay/reconciliation preserves uncertain effects without repeating science.
    Ordinary throughput stays within a stated unattended envelope through owned
    retention/allocation renewal. Focused scenarios distinguish automatic recovery
    from justified operator intervention; no new generic retry framework is required.

A01 evidence: package import/health/receipt never means science or workflow passed;
    completion report labels each proof layer and retains failed results honestly.
A02 binding: preserve exact identities and independently chosen numeric tolerances;
    loaded-state/reference tests survive package integration without tuned goldens.
A03 diagnostics: bounded useful reasons, no raw-input dump or broad ValueError-to-
    user-error conversion; oversized/nested/private-cause fixtures preserve semantics.
A04 packaging: complete imported/resource closure, not just hashes of a subset;
    missing resource and forbidden ambient import tests fail installed qualification.
A05 ownership: one local core for shared behavior, existing operational owners;
    consumer/import review rejects duplicate signer/downloader/journal and silent
    old/new fallback. Future migration must remove the displaced implementation.
A06 binding: three sibling contract fixtures retain their different scientific
    semantics; core contains no peaks/solvent/nucleus/atom-map policy or schema.
A07 binding/result: preserve coordinate/stereo limits through publication; renderer
    tests prevent stronger stereochemical claims than the scientific result supports.
A08 binding/source: explicit units and conversion evidence; core passes bytes and
    provenance without inferring units; wrong/ambiguous-unit cases cannot be repaired
    by transport, source parsing or implicit core defaults.
A09 capacity: execute actual maximum advertised workload and result envelope;
    synthetic admission and a healthy core cannot certify native/model capacity.
A10 retention: durable notes and identified evidence, no raw transcripts or abandoned
    compatibility trees; cleanup preserves the only supporting observations.

A11 service/composition: a documented safe stop is not unattended resilience;
    review rejects routine manual restart, repeated limit increases or fresh-root
    replacement as normal recovery/throughput, and rejects blind workflow retries.

Resilience audit, 2026-10-22
---------------------------
Source review against new U27/A11; no tests or live outages were run for this audit.
This is an implementation assessment, not a new qualification campaign.

Gap 1: nmrnet_provider/service.py::ProviderService.run/_watch stops the service
when work or Hello raises, and _work_loop has no transient-outage recovery loop.
Its class contract explicitly stops on outages. docs/provider.md describes this
honestly, but temporary dependency failures still require operator restart or
recovery. SECS runtime.py::run_execution at 7356c354368f2355d3ab5cef00cf48c67cea9c2c
shows the relevant narrower backoff precedent. Correction belongs at the operation
and service owners, with distinct read, definite-refusal and uncertain-write
dispositions; do not retry an entire Attempt or rerun science.

Gap 2: docs/provider.md, "Retained history and a full store", specifies keep-all
retention: confirmed successes/failures consume maximum_records forever. Normal
throughput eventually stops and requires an operator to raise the limit and restart.
tools/provider.sh::provider_host_monitor also stops at native request capacity
and asks for a fresh installation root. These are separate resource owners and
both constrain unattended operation. Define the intended operating envelope and
owned safe retention/renewal behavior before choosing a mechanism. Increasing
limits, dropping unresolved evidence or blindly recycling allocations is not a fix.

Existing strengths: configurable exchange, preparation, polling and settlement
budgets; explicit retained terminal requests and read-only reconciliation states;
separate scientific execution and publication; exact host-owned retirement; original
and independent cleanup causes retained in protected diagnostics. These support
resilience but do not establish automatic recovery. Commissioner/receiver delivery
is a separate claim from operator failure.json. Required follow-up should address
the concrete recovery and resource-lifetime gaps, reusing existing focused tests.

Implementation order after the resilience audit
----------------------------------------------
The maintainer's unattended-operation requirement supplies the concrete consumer
need missing from the earlier status-only assessment. U27/A11 supersede that
assessment: supported stop behavior must remain honestly reported until repaired,
but documenting it is not closure. No scientific work needs repeating for these
control-loop changes. Runtime checkpoint for this design:7c9019e.

1. Retry reviewed startup-admission and idle-discovery GET outages in the existing
   service task. Keep admission.py's bounded one-shot walks. Retire original API
   I/O before classifying its final evidence; no replacement transport or detached
   retry task. The API owner permits safe GET repetition after selected gateway
   failures and explicitly recognized network/timeouts. This does not establish
   transience or admit an error body: proxies may return HTML during maintenance.
   Certificate, authentication, explicit wrong environment, malformed success,
   capacity and ownership failures still refuse. Status supplies no mutation
   permission or authoritative API observation.
   Re-enter the whole admission/discovery cycle, including local capacity and
   cursor=None, without carrying a selection across an outage. Allocation,
   controller.run and all mutations remain outside this retry scope.
   Configure capped exponential delay, jitter and stop-responsive waits. Reset
   backoff only after the complete read cycle succeeds. Keep constant-size current
   outage context separate from terminal failures, and use the existing protected
   process renderer for retry/recovery notices. A reporting or retirement failure
   retains the original outage. Tests first in the existing API/service/process
   lanes: recover and execute once; restart pagination; stop during backoff;
   held cleanup forbids another request; permanent failures do not loop.
2. Address initial/periodic Hello independently. Its current task still stops the
   service on outage, so step1 cannot establish general outage survival. Recompute
   the current complete announcement after confirmed retirement; no blind replay
   of a stale announcement or use of GET retry permission for a POST.
3. Give active execution an explicit finite observation-gap policy. No new phase,
   execution entry, accepted result or publication follows without fresh observed
   authority. Grace expiry/observed stop retires the original session; never restart
   interpretation or science. Uncertain START/terminal writes keep their existing
   replay/reconciliation paths. Uncertain progress remains with its original
   API/executor owner; do not replay obsolete progress or route mutations through
   the read-retry loop.
   Separately classify per-Job budget exhaustion at its owning binding. Return
   a legitimate terminal failure, not a catch-all bad-input result, only after
   the original work can be confirmed stopped. Continue fresh service admission
   after confirmed publication and complete retirement; unresolved effects or
   cleanup still hold it. No whole-Attempt retry converts a budget failure into
   another scientific execution.
4. Define the unattended history/disk and native-request envelope at their current
   owners before changing retention or renewal. Preserve unresolved obligations,
   exact replay and required scientific/operator evidence. No implicit deletion,
   root rotation or process replacement hides an unsettled owner.
   Implement the resulting owned retention/renewal policy; defining an envelope
   alone does not close U27/A11 or establish unattended ordinary throughput.

This is a phased implementation plan, not proof of these behaviors. Two independent
read-only owner/design reviews informed step1; both identified Hello as a separate
service-fatal path and required fresh complete reads plus settled original I/O.
Re-use the maintained focused lanes; no new harness or exhaustive outage campaign.

X01 now applies: persistent provider and terminal reconciliation in P3; explicit
    outcome precedence for unordered event batches and uncertain replies.
X02 applies as serial admission per execution owner/no implicit queue. Composition
may schedule independent owners; duplicate-claim exclusion is required. Model RNG
and daemon scheduling frameworks remain outside provider-core's initial contract.
X03 applies to required durable terminal obligations, not a new general task queue;
    bounded record growth and atomic replacement capacity tested before admission.
X04 applies at actual API/store/control transports: full exchange deadlines and
    released sockets/helpers, including trickle and interrupted-response tests.
X05 preparation is required by 003 but stays outside core. Core must preserve its
    provenance/limits and cannot claim native-reader qualification from wheel hashes.
X06 ensemble/GPU/precision/performance campaigns remain outside this package change;
    no core device or averaging policy. Existing scientific distinctions survive.
X07 sibling instructions are evidence, not imported authority. This checkout's
    containment, permission and explicit-path commit rules remain in force.

Implementation: first installed input slice, 2026-10-18
-----------------------------------------------------
Implemented standalone provider-core 0.1.0, its portable execution guard and exact
Job-input verifier. Frozen feed identity and immutable source bytes cross the
boundary; payloads are excluded from repr and parser failures do not echo private
values. NMRNet's direct adapter consumes verified bytes through existing geometry
admission, without a second scientific parser or malformed-input reinterpretation.
This internal binding is not a live API submission route or Attempt controller.

Tests were written and run red before each implementation: missing job_input
module first, then missing submission adapter. The installed tests now pass:
  make test/unit/provider-core: 9 tests, including normative/adversarial subcases.
  make test/contract/provider-core: 4 tests (installed files/imports, guard, empty suite).
  make test/integration/provider-core: 3 real NMRNet adapter tests.
  make test/unit/provider: 22 existing/new input and launcher tests.
  make test/repository/provider-core: 36 focused convention/inventory/guard tests.
All ran in offline containers. Build/runtime environments are separate, source
and dependency mounts read-only, wheel installation hash-checks the retained
setuptools input, and runtime imports are outside the checkout. Current build
dependency advisory observations and limitations are in requirements/provider-core.md.
Package/runtime/test roots enter the existing source, guard, lint and cache checks.
The normal test aggregate includes the new unit, contract and consumer lanes.

Independent reviews covered input ownership/privacy, U/A evidence and contained
lane grants. Corrections included reaching actual decoded-payload hashing in a
test, capturing live traceback privacy, exact installed file inventory, and
rejecting intermediate symlinked consumer-test directories. Focused lint/format
was applied in the existing checker image. A temporary focused-check script was
replaced, at maintainer direction, with test/repository/provider-core and removed.
Adding actual-source assertions exposed 14 pre-existing broad import-order findings
in test_prediction_model.py/test_prediction_release.py. The broad assertion remains
unchanged; the core lane selects its actual package/tool/adapter import-order check
and all three real source visibility/tree/mount assertions, not just detector fakes.
No broad aggregate, network authentication, async lifecycle, live API/GUI or new
scientific qualification is claimed by this slice. Those remaining P2-P5 contracts
stay open; installed Job-input consistency does not authenticate a network peer.

Exact signing slice, 2026-10-18
------------------------------
Added the pure RFC 9421 request constructor, deriving parameters/components from
the pinned API profile and adapting Magnet's exact-target signing precedent.
The package README links both owners precisely. No client, clock, nonce generator
or retry policy is hidden in signing; the eventual API operation owner supplies
time and a fresh nonce. Body None and empty bytes remain distinct. Identity repr
does not reveal credentials or key material, and HTTPX target rewriting rejects.
Tests ran red with the missing signing module before implementation. Independent
verification reconstructs signature bytes and checks actual emitted headers;
three reviewers covered protocol, privacy/admission and contained installation.
Review found IPvFuture bracket syntax could become a different DNS destination;
its regression ran red, then admission was corrected while retaining IPv6 support.
The installed unit suite now has 15 passing tests, with five contract and 36 focused
repository checks passing. This qualifies local request construction, not server
acceptance, transport retirement or any API mutation.

Bounded async transport slice, 2026-10-18
----------------------------------------
Added a serial HTTPS exchange owner using one fresh client per exchange. This
removes pooled-connection reuse ambiguity without adding a scheduler. Composition
supplies verified TLS trust, exchange duration and a separate finite retirement
grace; ambient proxies/auth and redirects are disabled. Raw response bytes are
bounded before handoff. Success requires response/client closure. Failed or
unfinished retirement retains its task and prevents another exchange; an explicit
retire operation observes later closure without losing the original failure.
Server mutation certainty remains the API operation owner's responsibility.

Tests ran red for the absent transport, then reproduced three review findings:
cleanup timeout hiding a read failure, repeated retirement erasing prior evidence,
and cancellation before the worker's first instruction leaving ownership stuck.
All were corrected. Original private causes, interruption and cleanup evidence
remain distinct; public exception rendering does not contain request/secret text.
Controlled cancellation-resistant I/O proves bounded return and retained ownership.

Initial focused evidence: 23 installed unit tests, 7 integration tests (four
finite-loopback TLS cases plus three real NMRNet adapter cases), five installed
contracts and 36 repository checks passed before extending connection coverage.
TLS tests cover successful closure, no redirect follow, headers/body stalls,
trickle deadline, cancellation and incomplete-response failure.
Peer-side closure and exact failure causes are asserted; unexpected fixture task
errors propagate. These are offline contained tests, not deployed API proof. The
next functional slice must join signing/transport to the real Job-input and Upload
contracts, after the unresolved transport defect below is corrected.

Current checkpoint: NOT qualified for transport use. Added TLS-handshake stall
cases reproduce two failures in test/integration/provider-core: cancellation and
deadline return without the peer observing socket closure. httpcore 1.0.9's
_backends/anyio.py::AnyIOStream.start_tls catches Exception, excluding cancellation;
_async/connection.py registers its connection only after TLS completion. Thus
HTTPX client closure is insufficient evidence for a pre-handshake raw stream.
Exact retained wheel sources support this diagnosis; current upstream source:
https://github.com/encode/httpcore/blob/1.0.9/httpcore/_backends/anyio.py
https://github.com/encode/httpcore/blob/1.0.9/httpcore/_async/connection.py
The fixture initially obscured this with teardown waiting for active connections
before cancelling handlers; teardown now closes handlers before waiting for the
server and has finite waits. The two actual socket regressions remain red.
No backend workaround has been implemented. Proposed correction is explicit
stream ownership through httpcore's public network-backend/pool interfaces,
avoiding private HTTPX pool mutation or an untracked patched wheel. The maintainer
requested this checkpoint commit before the exhaustive U/A audit and revision.

TLS ownership correction, 2026-10-18
-----------------------------------
The red checkpoints e34540c and 5bbc586 are superseded by an explicit stream
owner using HTTPcore's public AsyncNetworkBackend/AsyncNetworkStream interfaces.
A TCP stream is retained before TLS upgrade and released only after successful
closure. Interrupted upgrade therefore leaves the raw stream available to final
cleanup. The earlier description of pool registration was imprecise: the pool
registers its connection wrapper before TLS; the wrapper does not retain the raw
stream when TLS upgrade is interrupted. The exact 1.0.9 sources above explain
why closing the HTTPX client alone did not suffice.

HTTPX still constructs the signed buffered request. Its method, raw URL target,
headers and content pass to a fresh HTTPcore pool with no retries or keepalive.
HTTPcore is now an explicit package dependency; the exact 1.0.9 wheel already
belongs to the reviewed locked closure. No dependency bytes changed. Native I/O
errors remain private causes; the public failure contract is ExchangeFailed.

Focused contained evidence: test/unit/provider-core passes 24 tests, including
raw-stream retention across cancelled TLS and failed closure; actual loopback
TLS cancellation and aggregate-deadline regressions now observe peer closure.
The integration lane additionally verifies an Ed25519 signature from the GET
request received by the TLS peer, including escaped query and Host authority.
This does not establish deployed API acceptance or body-digest wire coverage.
Installed-contract coverage now imports all operational modules outside the
checkout and excludes scientific/repository/test modules. The 36 focused
repository checks and package Ruff checks pass. Independent read-only review
found no runtime defect; its source-description correction was applied, followed
by a second review with no new actionable findings.

U/A audit disposition for this correction: U07 gains actual TLS retirement and
composed-signature evidence; U08/U14/U17/U23/A01 gain socket ownership through
handshake cancellation. U09/U18/U22/U19 status prose now distinguishes implemented
transport from unfinished API/lifecycle work. A04 gains clean installed imports
of every operational module. Scientific behavior, upstream code and sibling
repositories remain untouched. Total Upload work budgets (U13), recipient-visible
scientific warnings (U24), and consuming protected transport causes in recipient
projection (U23) remain obligations of their later owning slices. This correction
does not close the package or note 003.
Final focused rerun: test/integration/provider-core passes eight tests (including
the composed signature), and test/contract/provider-core passes five tests.

Signed Job-input operation slice, 2026-10-18
------------------------------------------
ProviderAPI now owns one signed, bodyless Job-input GET. It validates the catalog
key before any exchange, uses a fresh nonce/time, admits the exact success HTTP
headers, and delegates byte binding to verify_job_input. Feed authentication is
an explicit caller precondition; FeedInputFacts alone proves shape, not authority.
Composition retains transport retirement authority. No retries or scientific work
are introduced. The API routes.py::JOB_INPUT_READ_ROUTE and security/
analysis_kind_refs.py at I govern the target, ceiling and catalog-key grammar.
Package README links these exact sources and explains their role.

ApiFailure keeps operation/status and private original response/cause. Cancellation
remains CancelledError and leaves operation context in last_failure. Review caught
that HTTP-number-only errors lost useful API meaning. A regression ran red before
adding a bounded known-problem projection: reviewed type/status/code combinations
map to local explanations, while arbitrary title/detail remain protected. Server
request IDs remain structured correlation, not interpolated public text. Unknown,
malformed, duplicate or mismatched problem documents retain the original private
response and a generic HTTP explanation. Sources: I:security/problems.py and
request_ids.py; the 4096-byte bound and grammar belong to that API boundary.
This is not yet the recipient-rendering completion required by P4.

Tests ran red for absent api.py, then green; the diagnostic regression separately
ran red for missing structured problem facts. Final focused contained lanes:

Ruff passes. The finite TLS peer now accepts a response payload so integration
can exercise correct and substituted Job identities through real transport. It
observes socket closure and exact transmitted route; signature-presence checking
here is not cryptographic server verification. The separate transport signature
test supplies that local witness; neither establishes deployed API acceptance.

Independent protocol/error reviews found no further actionable runtime findings
after the diagnostic correction. A separate full U01-U26/A01-A10 scope review
required an explicit feed-authentication precondition and current package guidance;

Upload membership and grant API slice, 2026-10-18
-----------------------------------------------
ProviderAPI now exposes uploads(job_ref) and read_grant(job_ref, upload_ref), using
its existing signer/transport owner. Both are bodyless; membership GET has no
query, and grant POST has no Content-Type or Content-Digest. The route-owned
response ceilings are 2097152 and 65536 bytes. upload_response.py owns exact wire
admission and immutable Job-bound membership/grant observations. It preserves
pending hashes, full ordered descriptions and resource identity independently of
content identity. Grant Job context comes from the request, not an echoed field.

Wire sources at I: provider/routes.py::JOB_UPLOAD_SET_READ_ROUTE and
JOB_UPLOAD_READ_CAPABILITY_ROUTE; provider/schemas/job_upload_set_read_response.v1.
schema.json; wire/upload_read_capability_response.py; wire/upload_transfer_schemas.py;
json_datetime.py; upload_store_target.py. These own membership limits, exact fields,
nullable hash, 359-character URL bound, 2048-character bearer and canonical UTC
expiry. The named records are inert observations: no store request, current-source
freeze, configured-origin admission or byte-verification claim is made here.
Those effects belong to the next Job-scoped acquisition slice.

The grant POST may extend retention before its reply is lost. ApiFailure retains
issuance_may_have_committed and a safe note on uncertain failures, including
cancelled/failed transport and malformed replies. No implicit retry occurs. This
is evidence about this call only, not reconciliation of an earlier grant. Reference
validation and signing failures precede transport handoff. A busy/locally refused
transport is conservatively uncertain; API code does not inspect transport internals
to manufacture dispatch certainty.

Review corrected overly broad uncertainty and useful-reason loss. Pinned I:
security/problems.py::_PROBLEM_SEND_EFFECT_DEFAULTS establishes no domain change
for recognised authentication failures, but explicitly makes no assertion for
generic authorization/not-found/conflict categories. I:operations/errors.py::
ProviderJobUploadReadNotFinalized and ProviderJobUploadReadPurgeInProgress explicitly
promise no new link; provider_job_upload_read_capability.py raises those before
issuance. Only these admitted exact 409 meanings and reviewed 401 meanings narrow
this grant's uncertainty. Local explanations tell the caller to finalize the
existing Upload, or seek another authorized source after Project purge. Arbitrary
server detail stays private. A review suggestion to treat all 403/404 replies as
no issuance was rejected against the exact source; the reviewer corrected it.

Tests-first evidence: missing Upload methods failed before implementation. The
reviewed-refusal test then failed before certainty/409 diagnostics were corrected.
Focused contained lanes pass: 39 unit, 11 integration, five installed-contract and
36 repository tests. Actual TLS tests cover membership/grant observation and
cancelled grant with one received request, retained uncertainty/transport cause and
peer-side closure. Maximum membership exercises all 64 full descriptions; pending
hashes, ordering, duplicates, foreign identities, invalid scalar/date/number/schema,
malformed/oversized JSON and secret-safe failures are covered. Installed inventory
includes the wire module. These remain local tests, not deployed API/store proof.

Independent protocol, failure and full U/A reviews found no further actionable
code/test issue after correction. Applicable U05/U07-U11/U13-U17/U23/U25/U26 and
A01/A03-A05 have explicit boundary witnesses; U18/U22 public guidance is updated.

from the preceding slice. Total transfer budgets, membership refresh/invalidation,

Final documentation recheck found no new actionable issue.

Job-scoped acquisition and shared budgets, 2026-10-18
---------------------------------------------------
JobUploads is the sole source-session owner: inspect current membership, select a
reference, admit source size, refresh the whole observation, obtain fresh access,
bind grant facts and destination, then verify the complete bytes. Per-source size
and remaining total byte allowance are admitted before grant issuance. Observed
membership changes or any failed acquisition invalidate selection; explicit
inspection is required before another attempt. No byte cache, retry loop or
parallel source worker is introduced. Pending hashes may finalize through the
grant; changes observed in refreshed membership require reselection. The API does
not supply an atomic freeze after the last observation, and this code makes none.

Explicit UploadLimits bound each source, total consumed store-body bytes, request
count and session duration. Inspection/refresh/grant/store calls share the request
and time limits from source-owner construction. Metadata retains its route-specific
body ceiling; it is bounded in aggregate by request count, not charged to the
store-body byte counter. HttpTransport accepts one synchronous byte-accounting
callback, invoked before rejecting an oversized chunk. Failed/cancelled consumed
bytes remain charged. An offending chunk can take the counter over the allowance;
it is recorded and rejected, not forgotten. This is application-body accounting,
not an OS traffic quota. No late verified-byte handoff is permitted after deadline.
Hashing remains synchronous under the per-source byte bound; the final clock check
rejects its late result rather than claiming preemptive CPU interruption.

All returned bytes are immutable and length/SHA-256 verified. Private intermediate
bytes remain in bounded session memory; no plaintext staging files are created.
Configured canonical store origin and exact API Upload path must match before the
bearer is attached. Store requests contain no provider signature, request body,
content headers or Range. HTTP 200 and singleton no-store/octet-stream/exact-length/
Content-Digest headers are checked independently of actual byte hashing. Original
private response/cause evidence is retained for diagnostics; public errors do not
render bearers or source bytes. API/store transports stay independently supplied
and composition-owned. The transport's retirement_confirmed property reports its
actual unresolved task/cleanup state; source work refuses unresolved store cleanup.

Provenance/rationale: I:upload_transfer_http.py::_read_response_start and
upload_transfer_http_contract.py own the exact store method/path/header contract.
S:src/secs_inference/provider/{job_api,upload_download}.py at the pinned S revision
is the source-ordering precedent. The local implementation does not import or copy
sibling code; it adds explicit async session accounting and existing transport
retirement composition. Exact links and public method contracts are in package
README. Neither tokens nor a metadata receipt establish membership freshness or
actual bytes; this separation motivates the ordering and independent checks.

Tests-first failures: absent uploads.py; verification completing after the session
deadline; and missing confirmed-issuance evidence after a successful grant followed
by a failed local/store check. Review found the latter two, regressions reproduced
them, and fixes now reject late results and retain grant_issued plus safe context.
Caller cancellation stays CancelledError, with protected original evidence retained.
A grant-time timeout keeps ApiFailure's possible-issuance fact without claiming
confirmed issuance. A later failed transfer cannot erase successful issuance.

Focused contained evidence covers exact-byte success and pending-hash finalization;
foreign target refusal before bearer dispatch; changed observations; failed grants
with changed/unchanged explicit reinspection; expired/mismatched grants; same-length
hash corruption; shared request budget; overlap refusal; retirement gating; final
verification deadline; and consumed failed bytes. Separate real TLS API/store peers
prove signed API calls, bearer-only store calls, socket closure, partial transfer
cancellation/deadline accounting and oversized-chunk charging. These establish local
composition, not deployed API/store availability or an actual sibling migration.
Installed inventory/import checks include uploads.py; shared test doubles remain
in fakes, successes in normative, faults in adversarial and sockets in integration.

Independent protocol/failure/full-U&A reviews converged after the deadline and
issuance-evidence corrections. U05/U07-U11/U13-U17/U23/U25/U26 and A01/A03-A05 have
behavioral witnesses; U18/U22 documentation is current. Scientific, Make, dependency
and preservation dispositions remain unchanged. P3 Attempt persistence/recovery,
P4 recipient delivery and scientific composition, and P5 adoption/deployed end-to-end
qualification remain unfinished; local acquisition completion does not close them.
Final focused validation: 53 unit tests (44 adversarial and nine normative), 14
integration, five installed-contract and 36 repository checks pass; package Ruff
passes. Only affected lanes were rerun after the final corrections.

ProviderAPI.read_attempt(execution_attempt_ref, job_ref=...) now performs the

states without folding Job cancellation/closure into Attempt terminalization.

closed/cancelled, and the response has no terminal content identity. A read cannot
establish which result/failure bytes committed or whether science ever started.

Generic bounded JSON decoding moved from upload_response.py to response_json.py,
shared by the two actual schema owners. ResponseRejected replaces the Upload-only
exception name throughout core; no unused compatibility alias was retained.

2026-10-18 P3 start protocol: exact retained requests and uncertain admission
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
start_body(Job, key) builds immutable request bytes with an explicit caller key.
ProviderAPI.start_attempt accepts retained bytes, validates the 4096-byte exact
request schema before dispatch and sends those same bytes with fresh signing.
Expected Job/kind/provider bind the returned AttemptAdmission. Terminal replay
states are admitted as observations, never a trigger to run scientific work.
Persistence before first send and before execution is the lifecycle owner's next
obligation; this protocol layer does not itself provide durable recovery.

Authority and rationale: I:nmr_api/provider/schemas/execution_attempt_start_request.
v1.schema.json and execution_attempt_start_response.v1.schema.json specify wire
facts; I:nmr_api/provider/routes.py::EXECUTION_ATTEMPT_START_ROUTE and
I:nmr_api/operations/execution_attempt_start.py specify unchanged replay and
replay-before-new-admission ordering. Generating a new key can create a different
logical start; retaining the original body prevents reconstruction from changing
its facts. Fresh request authentication must not change those facts.
All I references use the previously pinned 6a163b7 revision, with complete links
in the package README and source ownership comments.

ApiFailure.attempt_mutation_may_have_committed preserves uncertainty for this send
on transport/cancellation/envelope/parser failures. I:nmr_api/security/problems.py
marks admitted401 and the selected409 start codes as no_domain_change for THIS
send: provider_attempt_key_conflict, job_provider_attempt_limit_reached and
job_attempt_limit_reached. Generic403/404/503 and other409 remain uncertain. A later
refusal cannot clear the retained uncertainty of an earlier failed send. Grant
issuance evidence remains separate. Original response and cause stay protected.

Tests first failed on the missing start API/request module. The contained package
lanes now pass 64 unit, five installed-contract and 17 integration tests; Ruff
passes. Exact legal whitespace/order survive explicit replay; real TLS tests
independently verify received body bytes, SHA-256 and Ed25519 signatures, fresh
authentication, terminal replay and cancellation/socket closure. Invalid requests
send nothing; malformed/foreign success and failed exchanges cannot imply absence
of an admitted Attempt. No broad scientific/repository suite was run.

Independent protocol, failure and full-U/A disposition reviews found no actionable
implementation issues. This closes only start's protocol boundary. Progress and
terminal publication, persistence/recovery, NMRNet composition, recipient delivery
and deployed/adoption qualification remain open under the unchanged full goal.

introduced. Composition still owns observation freshness and safe code vocabulary.

2026-10-18 P3 terminal wire boundary: retain and reconcile the chosen outcome
-------------------------------------------------------------------------
complete_body/fail_body build exact terminal requests; complete_attempt/fail_attempt
validate retained bodies before dispatch and send them unchanged. Completion binds
the returned Attempt/schema/hash/length to decoded opaque result bytes; failure
binds exact code/message. Separate result-reference validation does not confuse
resource identity with content hash. Both acknowledgements preserve committed time
and boolean replay status. Allowed scalar text, controls and whitespace are not
normalized. Shape validation does not decide which diagnostic text is publishable;
composition must protect confidential evidence before building a failure request.

Authority: I:nmr_api/provider/schemas/execution_attempt_{complete,fail}_{request,
response}.v1.schema.json, I:nmr_api/provider/routes.py terminal route declarations,
I:nmr_api/operations/canonical_content.py and execution_attempt_outcome.py. All
refer to pinned I 6a163b7; package README links each exact schema. The service's
exact existing-result/failure comparison explains the retention/replay choice.
I:nmr_api/security/problems.py explicitly assigns current-send no change to outcome
expiry, opposite-outcome and terminal replay mismatch codes. The client admits
those precise meanings but never clears earlier uncertainty from them. Unknown,
malformed, interrupted or cancelled publication remains uncertain with protected
original evidence. No operation retries, switches terminal choice or runs science.

Tests first failed on missing terminal builders. Focused installed-container lanes
pass 76 unit and 21 integration tests. Unit cases include opaque non-UTF8 results,

requests, foreign acknowledgements and earlier uncertainty followed by refusal.

Unicode failure scalars through admission/signing/acknowledgement. They also verify
SHA-256 and Ed25519 from received requests, fresh signing on explicit identical
resend after connection loss, and cancellation/socket closure for both operations.
The scripted peer does not implement database commits; its replay acknowledgement
is client-handling evidence, not proof of server idempotency or durable recovery.
